Etapa Clasificatoria - Qualification stage:
Tie-breaks used in this stage:
1 Head-to-head points.
1.a Head-to-head goal difference.
1.b Head-to-head goals for.
1.c Head-to-head away goal rule.
Repeat procedure 1 if necessary.
2 Position table in the group.
2.d Goal difference.
2.e Goals-for.
2.f Away goal rule.
Repeat procedure 2 if necessary.
2.g Lot.
3 Tie-breaks used for tables of second-place or third-place teams.
3.a Goal difference.
3.b Goals for.
3.c Away goal rule.
3.d Lot.

CSyD Linies v. CSyD Central Ballester: one point dedcuted to both teams.
Due to the covid 19 pandemic, AFA ended the season and annulled the relegations on April 27.
However, on February 8, 2021, AFA proclaimed CA Claypole as champion and the promotions of CA Claypole and CA Atlas as part of the 2019/2020 season.
